# Skills · C · part 20

913 entries, catalogue-maintenance to catering-services-service-plan. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **catalogue-maintenance**: Inspect Arda catalogue items and suppliers, prepare changes for review, and execute approved changes. Use for catalogue lookup and maintenance through the selected Arda environment. ([Arda-cards/agent-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/Arda-cards/agent-plugin-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/arda-cards-operations/skills/catalogue-maintenance))
- **catalogue-menu-art**: Generating the small menu-art thumbnails a catalogue entry, variant or mix shows in place of its text header, using the generate.py and menuart.py scripts in this skill. Use when adding or changing anything the catalogue loads, when a header should show art instead of a name, when the panel's size or colours change, or when the art looks wrong. ([rc4l/ForkUnderA](https://github.com/rc4l/ForkUnderA/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/catalogue-menu-art) · ★3)
- **catalogue-merchandising-review**: Use when the merchant asks how to merchandise the catalogue, which products or categories deserve attention, which products are slow movers, how current stock or sale pricing affects product placement, or which product pages need practical improvements. Produces a merchant-friendly WooCommerce catalogue merchandising review using existing analytics and product tools, with aggregated data only, no customer PII, no forecasts, and no suggestions to build new tools. ([woocommerce/woocommerce-claude](https://github.com/woocommerce/woocommerce-claude/tree/HEAD/plugins/hey-woo/skills/catalogue-merchandising-review) · ★33 · 3 copies)
- **catalogue-mix**: Adding a mix (gameplay mod) to catalogue/remix/ — the shared pool, group semantics, which experiences should offer it, and when a mix needs a cfg of its own. Use whenever adding a gameplay mod that layers on top of an experience, or changing which experiences can be played with one. ([rc4l/ForkUnderA](https://github.com/rc4l/ForkUnderA/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/catalogue-mix) · ★3)
- **catalogue-of-life-taxonomic-resolution-filtering**: Use when biological-source metadata contains free-text organism names that must be resolved against the Catalogue of Life and filtered to species-level completeness before joining structures to sources. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/catalogue-of-life-taxonomic-resolution-filtering))
- **catalogue-page**: Turn a list of finished pieces into a catalogue and price list, as a spreadsheet plus a page ready to print for a fair or a gallery. Use when the user asks for a catalogue, a price list, an inventory of work, or descriptions of their pieces for selling. ([vadimchernets/calling-kits](https://github.com/vadimchernets/calling-kits/tree/HEAD/plugins/artist-kit/skills/catalogue-page) · ★1)
- **catalogue-produits-crm**: Utiliser quand l'utilisateur gère son catalogue de produits ou services CRM — « ajoute cette prestation au catalogue », « change le prix de ce produit », « liste mes produits », « ce produit n'existe plus ». Référentiel produits/services qui alimente devis et factures, pas le stock restaurant. ([PrendsTaPart/Plugin-Claude-MCP-BraindCode-](https://github.com/PrendsTaPart/Plugin-Claude-MCP-BraindCode-/tree/HEAD/rapidocrm/skills/catalogue-produits-crm) · ★8)
- **catalogue-report**: Prepares a TV Slim catalogue report for a TV or box whose packages the catalogue does not know yet, to be posted by its owner as a GitHub issue on jolabs40/TVSlim. Use when someone wants to help TV Slim support their device, or asks what the unknown packages on their TV are. ([jolabs40/TVSlim](https://github.com/jolabs40/TVSlim/tree/HEAD/plugins/tvslim/skills/catalogue-report) · ★39)

- **catalysis-analysis**: Use when the task is to analyze catalysis-relevant quantities from DFT results, including adsorption energies, d-band center estimates, reaction barriers from NEB images, mode-specific catalyst-set ranking, adsorption or pathway selectivity comparisons, and compact markdown reports from finished calculations. Supports VASP, QE, and ABINIT-style energy inputs. ([chatmaterials/catalysis-analysis](https://github.com/chatmaterials/catalysis-analysis/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **catena**: Catena patch-based version control reference. ALWAYS use this skill when interacting with Catena — running the `catena` CLI, creating/proposing/accepting patches, working with the trunk, releases, reviews, or `.catena` workspaces. Catena is NOT Git: it is online-first and patch-based with a maturity lifecycle (DraftLocal → DraftSynced → DraftShared → Proposed → Accepted).
